# Skills · K · part 5

834 entries, kit-deadcode to knowledge-base-builder. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **kit-deadcode**: Dead-code gate for a theme kit — flag CSS classes and @keyframes that are defined but never applied. kit-lint catches dead TOKENS; this catches dead CLASSES and keyframes (a class defined in CSS but in no .tsx and used in no compound selector, or a keyframe with no animation consumer). Run after editing or removing a component/decoration, before accepting a kit. ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-deadcode) · ★1)
- **kit-demo-states**: Static gate — every state a kit wrapper EXPOSES must be EXHIBITED by the demo. For each kit it scans component dirs for state props (`disabled?:` at top level or in an items def, `error?:`), then requires a matching usage in that kit's App.tsx (tag attr, inline items entry, or a referenced items const), plus one disabled MenuItem in each menu host panel (menu/menubar/context). Kills the class where a styled/API-real state (disabled+checked, folding disabled, static error) has no demo instance — invisible to the eye, to kit-states, and to the kit-interact disabled checks (they only audit rende… ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-demo-states) · ★1)
- **kit-dev**: Fast local dev loop for claude-kit itself (the plugin source). start: worktree + launch instructions for `claude --plugin-dir` (plugin loads live from disk, no cache, no version bump). Iterate with /reload-plugins. ship: auto-bump semver + kit-task-pr-auto + one-command update of the installed plugin. ([jeiemgi/cckit](https://github.com/jeiemgi/cckit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-dev) · ★2)
- **kit-dev**: Як тут працювати, щоб не наступити на відомі граблі — методика B1–B8. Тригери — пишу бриф субагенту; комічу в спільній робочій копії; пишу перевірку/guard; пишу чи мутую тести; ділю/перенумеровую документ; скорочую інструкцію чи знімаю документ з обліку; вимірюю число для людини; оновлюю кеш плагіна; роблю рев'ю; приймаю процедуру пілотом; виправляю дефект. ([VSydorenko/SMP_V8StorageKit](https://github.com/VSydorenko/SMP_V8StorageKit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-dev) · ★2)
- **kit-digest**: Pre-digest long inputs (video transcripts, CI logs, big files, web pages) with the LOCAL model (mlx_lm.server) so the Claude session reads a short digest + pointer instead of the full content — token saving on every long read. ([jeiemgi/cckit](https://github.com/jeiemgi/cckit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-digest) · ★2)
- **kit-dispatch**: Fill-in dispatch templates for delegating work to subagents. Use whenever you are about to spawn a subagent (Agent tool) for research, implementation, refactoring, or verification/review — copy the matching template, fill every blank, then dispatch. Enforces the kit-delegation "派工三件套" (goal+context / acceptance criteria / report format). ([cholateio/multi-agent-starter](https://github.com/cholateio/multi-agent-starter/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-dispatch) · ★1)
- **kit-distinct**: Anti-reskin gate — flag a theme kit that is a structural copy (reskin) of a sibling rather than a native design. kit-lint checks token hygiene (a recolor passes it cleanly); this checks the visual layer was authored, not inherited. Run when accepting a new or heavily-changed kit, alongside kit-lint + kit-states. Usage targets a kit id, e.g. "check the &lt;kit-id&gt; kit is distinct". ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-distinct) · ★1)
- **kit-docs-writer**: Write and edit end-user documentation in the house voice: kind but robust, ADHD-oriented and scannable, ZERO analogies, beginner-inclusive but expert-skippable. Encodes the page template (title → one-line description → self-explanatory left-to-right diagram → body → next-section buttons) and the diagram discipline (interactive, left-to-right, self-explanatory, never repetitive). Use it to keep every docs page readable by people with low software knowledge without watering down the substance. ([jeiemgi/cckit](https://github.com/jeiemgi/cckit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-docs-writer) · ★2)
- **kit-doctor**: Use to diagnose a senior-dev-kit installation — component file counts, settings merge state, and version drift. Read-only. ([mtvrkan/senior-dev-kit](https://github.com/mtvrkan/senior-dev-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-doctor) · ★5)
- **kit-doctor**: Re-runnable Fieldbook health check. Verifies the Claude Code version against the kit's assumptions, that the hooks fire and core.hooksPath is set, that every hook script has a tracked settings.json registration (and every registration a script), that jq/python3/stat are present, that the doc-schema + index lint are clean, and that the install manifest matches what's on disk (flagging drift/missing files). Reports findings and SUGGESTS fixes but mutates nothing without consent. Use when hooks seem inert, after a Claude Code update, after a fresh clone, or any time you want to confirm the insta… ([armenr/modelmux](https://github.com/armenr/modelmux/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-doctor) · ★2)
- **kit-doctor**: Verifica que el plugin kit de claude-kit está instalado, habilitado y con sus componentes cargados. Usar cuando se quiera confirmar la instalación del kit o diagnosticar por qué un componente del kit no aparece. ([Garencio/Claude-Garencio](https://github.com/Garencio/Claude-Garencio/tree/HEAD/plugins/kit/skills/kit-doctor))
- **kit-effort-close**: Close an effort in ONE op — snapshot each sub-issue's diff BEFORE squash (work record), capture/judge/sync effort metrics, squash-merge the effort PR, close the parent + ALL native sub-issues, set the board Status=Done for each, garbage-collect the worktree/branch, run the optional knowledge-ingest hook, and finish with the kit-sync drift check. ([jeiemgi/cckit](https://github.com/jeiemgi/cckit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-effort-close) · ★2)
- **kit-effort-new**: Create an effort — a parent GitHub issue using the effort-model template (Goal / Scope / For agents / Verification) plus N native GitHub sub-issues linked via the sub-issues REST API, all added to the project board. The parent issue IS the plan. ([jeiemgi/cckit](https://github.com/jeiemgi/cckit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-effort-new) · ★2)
- **kit-effort-pr**: Open the ONE pull request for an effort — `effort/&lt;N&gt;` → base branch — with a rich human-facing review body plus a `## For agents` section listing the file paths/entry points. Title `[Role] [#N] &lt;title&gt;`. Inherits labels + milestone from the parent issue and moves it to In Review on the board. ([jeiemgi/cckit](https://github.com/jeiemgi/cckit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-effort-pr) · ★2)
- **kit-effort-start**: Start an effort — thin caller of `cckit effort start &lt;N&gt;`. Creates the `effort/&lt;N&gt;-&lt;slug&gt;` integration branch + isolated bootstrapped worktree from the base branch, then marks the parent issue In Progress on the board. Enforces the effort WIP limit (`effort.wipLimit`, default 2) — a new effort is refused, before anything is written, while that many are already in progress; `--force` starts anyway. Sub-issues later branch from this effort branch or commit directly on it. ([jeiemgi/cckit](https://github.com/jeiemgi/cckit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-effort-start) · ★2)
- **kit-entrance**: App 入场层门——app/theme/&lt;kit&gt;.md §入场 点名的 &lt;kit&gt;- keyframe 必须存在且被 animation 消费；跨套 reveal 块归一后逐字相同=抄袭 FAIL；每套 App.css 必须带 reduced-motion 门。改 App 入场/hero/reveal 或其 spec 后跑。 ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-entrance) · ★1)
- **kit-equality**: Cross-kit pinned-value conformance, one browser pass, five checks — (1) the 各 kit 同值 numbers (z-layer ladder, modal/drawer widths + viewport caps, NavigationMenu column, shell-frame geometry from app.md) are IDENTICAL across all kits, (2) every kit's rendered sidebar matches the canonical 面板清单 pinned in prompt/app/app.md, (3) every kit's anchored-popup list shows exactly 7 rows before scrolling — the invariant is the ROW COUNT, not the pixel height, so the gate opens a Select and divides the resolved popup-h by the rendered row height (a kit whose list-item-h drifts from its real row height s… ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-equality) · ★1)
- **kit-gc**: Garbage-collect the repo — prune worktrees whose PR already merged, delete merged local + remote branches, surface orphan/unpushed commits and stale stashes, and flag issues whose PR merged but stayed open. Dry-run by default; destructive steps require explicit confirmation. ([jeiemgi/cckit](https://github.com/jeiemgi/cckit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-gc) · ★2)
- **kit-glyph-center**: Asserts a leading marker/icon glyph on a title (modal titles, fieldset legends, accordion/collapsible triggers, panel titles) is vertically centered on its text across every kit. Catches the recurring "title 不居中 / sigil floats high" class. ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-glyph-center) · ★1)
- **kit-health**: Confirms the Clankbrain install worked. Run this right after setup or anytime you suspect something is broken. Triggers on "kit health", "is the kit installed", "kit check", "did install work", "verify kit", "health check kit". ([YehudaFrankel/clankbrain](https://github.com/YehudaFrankel/clankbrain/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-health) · ★16)
- **kit-hello**: Smoke-test skill for the starter kit. Confirms the plugin installed and skills load. Use when the user runs /kit-hello or asks to verify the starter kit installation. ([chrisblattman/claudeblattman](https://github.com/chrisblattman/claudeblattman/tree/HEAD/plugins/starter-kit/skills/kit-hello) · ★459)
- **kit-hover**: Field-family hover contract gate — the five field controls (Input, Select trigger, Combobox, Autocomplete, NumberField) must hover as ONE unit per kit, and a chevron must never carry its own hover. Real-mouse per kit on a fresh page (CDP forcePseudoState lies about pseudo-element hover and contaminates later reads), it snapshots the whole panel subtree's paint props (incl ::before/::after) and asserts (1) rest→hover response is uniform across the five controls within a kit — all respond or all stay quiet, the archetype itself is theme freedom — and (2) the panel renders IDENTICALLY whether th… ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-hover) · ★1)
- **kit-ingerir**: Alimenta o acervo pessoal de sites com material novo — prompts, componentes, efeitos, design systems, projetos inteiros ou catálogos servidos por MCP — classificando por conteúdo e escrevendo a ficha que torna a peça encontrável depois. Aceita arquivo, texto colado, pasta de projeto, registro shadcn ou servidor MCP. Use quando o usuário disser "guarda isso", "coloquei mais arquivos na pasta", "adicionei uns códigos", "conectei um MCP", "adiciona no acervo", "salva esse componente", "quero indexar esse projeto", mostrar um código ou prompt que queira reaproveitar, ou invocar /kit-ingerir. Use… ([firehagge-ui/HorusOS-Pro](https://github.com/firehagge-ui/HorusOS-Pro/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-ingerir) · ★1)
- **kit-interact**: Dynamic INTERACTION gate for the theme kits — drives the components the way a user would (tap on mobile, pop a toast sequence, click a trigger) and asserts behavior that a static screenshot can't see. Catches overlays that don't open on touch, an overlay/dialog wider than the phone viewport, a page that scrolls sideways on mobile, a toast stack whose newest is hidden behind older ones, a hovered toast stack that piles up instead of expanding readable (the px-length `--toast-offset-y` trap), a visible-but-inert over-limit toast, a pointer in the seam between expanded toasts collapsing the stac… ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-interact) · ★1)
- **kit-lint**: Acceptance check for a theme kit under src/kits/&lt;kit&gt; against the token contract (prompt/components/components.md §3). A kit's CSS must RESTATE NOTHING — every value comes from a token, every repeated recipe from a shared class or primitive — and must cover every contract group. Run after generating or heavily editing a kit, before accepting it. Usage targets a kit id, e.g. "lint the &lt;kit-id&gt; kit". ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-lint) · ★1)
- **kit-loop**: Turn one triaged Kit issue into a pull request - pick it, read its brief, implement it test-first on its own branch, open the PR - "/kit-loop", "/kit-loop 42", "run the Kit Loop". Run on demand from a clean checkout of the Kit's master; a session may also launch it as a subagent. ([Drago96/fullstack-kit](https://github.com/Drago96/fullstack-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-loop))
- **kit-modify**: Modify milky-kit itself from within a consuming project. Edits the kit checkout, commits + pushes to the kit's git remote, then realigns the current project against the updated rules. Use when the user notices a kit-level convention worth changing while working on a project — captures the change at the source without losing focus. ([milkyskies/milky-kit](https://github.com/milkyskies/milky-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-modify) · ★1)
- **kit-montar**: Monta um site novo combinando peças já validadas do acervo pessoal do usuário — templates, design systems, componentes, animações, menus, prompts e receitas — em vez de gerar tudo do zero. Acervo vazio: constrói do zero e oferece guardar. Recebe o briefing, decide cor e tipografia antes da identidade, **pergunta que tipo de hero o usuário quer** (tipográfico, editorial, vídeo de fundo, scroll cinemático, WebGL, carrossel) antes de escrever código, e gera a imagem ou o vídeo do hero pelo Higgsfield quando não há material. Escolhe as peças, porta de Vite para Next.js quando preciso, resolve pré… ([firehagge-ui/HorusOS-Pro](https://github.com/firehagge-ui/HorusOS-Pro/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-montar) · ★1)
- **kit-naming**: Cross-kit class-naming consistency, three checks. (1) For each component shared by all kits, the dominant block class (de-kit-prefixed) should be the same across kits. (2) Sub-part separator consistency — a leaf written `block-part` (single dash) in one kit while a sibling writes `block__part` (folded in from the former kit-part-naming gate). (3) Root-slot consistency — the component-local block on each `Base*.Root` element's className must match across kits. Catches the divergence kit-lint can't see (it checks token FORMAT within a kit, never whether names match across kits). ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-naming) · ★1)
- **kit-next**: The everyday cckit loop: find the next unblocked issue and pick it up, or fan the whole unblocked wave out to subagents. Wraps `cckit next` (what can I work on now?), `cckit start &lt;N&gt;` (isolated worktree + branch), `cckit copilot` (parallel fan-out), and `cckit watch` (gate + merge PRs). ([jeiemgi/cckit](https://github.com/jeiemgi/cckit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-next) · ★2)
- **kit-onboard**: Run the claude-kit two-tier onboarding interview — the wide-range GLOBAL profile (asked once in a lifetime, writes ~/.claude/kit.profile.&lt;user&gt;.json) and the per-PROJECT interview (one batched round, pre-filled from your profile + what's detected in this directory). Use AUTOMATICALLY when the user asks to onboard, set up the kit for the first time, "interview me", create my kit profile, configure a new project, or when /kit-init runs against a project with no kit profile yet. Pairs with /kit-add to stack modules (e.g. software) later. ([jeiemgi/cckit](https://github.com/jeiemgi/cckit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-onboard) · ★2)
- **kit-otimizar-3d**: Otimiza cena three.js, React Three Fiber ou WebGL cru para celular e máquina fraca — tiering de dispositivo, prewarm no loader para nada compilar no meio do scroll, render loop só quando visível, teto de DPR e de partícula/bloom, transformação de scroll na GPU, textura comprimida, e cena fora do bundle para robô. Use quando o usuário disser "otimiza o 3D", "a cena trava no celular", "está engasgando no scroll", "micro travadas", "deixa leve no mobile", "reduz o custo do WebGL", "o hero 3D está pesado", "optimise the 3D", "jank on scroll", ou antes de entregar qualquer projeto que carregue cen… ([firehagge-ui/HorusOS-Pro](https://github.com/firehagge-ui/HorusOS-Pro/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-otimizar-3d) · ★1)
- **kit-overflow-bleed**: 滚动容器出血带门——模态与抽屉里凡 overflow 非 visible 的容器，都不得裁掉它承载内容的盒外绘制（焦点环、骑在边界上的旋钮与花记）。改滑块旋钮尺寸、控件焦点环、模态体内衬后跑。 ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-overflow-bleed) · ★1)
- **kit-parity**: Cross-kit FUNCTIONAL coverage gate — flag a theme kit missing a small functional/a11y rule its siblings have (e.g. `:empty` to collapse an empty dropdown block, `text-overflow` ellipsis on long values, a styled scrollbar). kit-lint/kit-distinct/kit-states all PASS such a kit because the gap only shows when someone uses it. Checks kit-WIDE coverage + per-component mobile-adapt; rendered interaction states are left to kit-states/kit-interact. Run when accepting or QAing a kit, especially one authored from scratch. Usage targets all kits, or one kit id. ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-parity) · ★1)
- **kit-parse**: Baseline override authoring from raw kit text — parses a unit's kit into a candidate src/skills/overrides/&lt;slug&gt;.json for units that have no hand-tuned override yet. Demoted to untuned-unit baselines since the TDD transition (kit-tdd is the primary path); findings-only. ([Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim](https://github.com/Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-parse))
- **kit-plan-next**: Forward planning from the current state: run `cckit plan-next` to inventory what the kit can already do (skills, verbs, rules, docs) and propose what to build next — grounded in that inventory — then hand a chosen item off into `/kit-effort-new`. The proposed plan always carries a mandatory docs + README step. ([jeiemgi/cckit](https://github.com/jeiemgi/cckit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-plan-next) · ★2)
- **kit-prompt**: Entrevista sobre o site e gera um PROMPT DE CONSTRUÇÃO completo, no padrão dos prompts já validados do acervo — com stack, fontes, paleta, estrutura seção a seção, medidas exatas e proibições explícitas. O resultado é texto para você levar a outra ferramenta (v0, Lovable, Cursor, outro modelo) ou guardar; não é código escrito no projeto. Use quando o usuário pedir "gera um prompt", "monta o prompt pra esse site", "quero um prompt pra clínica", "preciso de um brief pra passar pro v0", ou invocar /kit-prompt. Se ele quiser o site construído aqui e agora, é a kit-montar, não esta. ([firehagge-ui/HorusOS-Pro](https://github.com/firehagge-ui/HorusOS-Pro/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-prompt) · ★1)
- **kit-qa**: One command that runs the whole theme-kit gate suite (every .claude/skills/kit-*/ gate that has a runnable check, plus theme-doc-sync) and prints a PASS/FAIL line per gate. Use after ANY kit CSS/component change and before accepting a kit — so the dynamic gates (kit-visual, kit-anim-sync, kit-interact, kit-glyph-center, …) can't be skipped the way they were when bugs shipped. Exits non-zero if any gate fails. ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-qa) · ★1)
- **kit-review**: Run the kit's profile-aware review on the current change set and record it for the Stop-hook gate. full profile -&gt; cross-model /codex:review; solo profile -&gt; fresh-context solo-reviewer subagent (state/time isolation only, disclosed to the user). Use when a change set needs its kit review, when the user says "review this", or when the Stop hook blocked the turn asking for a review. ([cholateio/multi-agent-starter](https://github.com/cholateio/multi-agent-starter/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-review) · ★1)
- **kit-scroll-rail**: 底轨覆盖门——横向滚动容器里意在铺满的装饰（底轨、外框线），滚到任一端后仍须盖住可视区。改 Tabs／Menubar／Toolbar 的滚动结构或底轨承载层后跑。 ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-scroll-rail) · ★1)
- **kit-security-sweep**: Full security audit of the project's apps. - **kit-setup**: One-time starter-kit setup: detects optional AI engines (Codex, Gemini), confirms each with you, writes the engines config, and optionally personalizes how Claude writes for you. - **kit-setup**: Install the parts of Senior Dev Kit a plugin cannot carry — the path-scoped rules and the deny list — into the user's Claude Code settings directory. ([mtvrkan/senior-dev-kit](https://github.com/mtvrkan/senior-dev-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-setup) · ★5)

- **kit-skip-review**: Record a skip of the kit's final-review Stop gate for the current session. - **kit-spec-cite**: Skin-doc citation gate — every backtick citation in prompt/components/theme/&lt;kit&gt;.md must resolve to something real in that kit's code (token as --&lt;kit&gt;-&lt;name&gt;, class, variant modifier, keyframe, SVG filter id, component prop, or full custom prop), so the docs can't drift into citing names the code no longer has (the --hanabi-hazard-tone class: the feature died in code, the 覆盖清单 kept citing it) or into aliases that aren't real names (`bright` for `text-bright` — the prompt-lint decode axis, mechanized). Pure static text pass, instant. Run after editing a skin doc or renaming/removing a token,… ([llwslc/proteus](https://github.com/llwslc/proteus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kit-spec-cite) · ★1)

- **klaussy-uninstall**: Use when the user wants klaussy's scaffolding out of a repository — removing the generated skills, hooks, settings, and ignore files that `klaussy init` wrote, and optionally the package itself. Previews everything before deleting, keeps hand-edited conventions docs by default, and never touches files klaussy doesn't own. ([steph-dove/klaussy-agents](https://github.com/steph-dove/klaussy-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/klaussy-uninstall) · ★16)
- **klaussy-update**: Use when the user wants to refresh klaussy-generated boilerplate (per-agent conventions, skills, settings, hooks) across every scaffolded agent after upgrading klaussy itself.
